  191: function sortDraws(draws = []) {
  192:   return [...draws].sort((a, b) => {
  193:     const aKey =
  194:       `${a.ymd}T${a.closeHour}T${a.drawId}`;
  195: 
  196:     const bKey =
  197:       `${b.ymd}T${b.closeHour}T${b.drawId}`;
  198: 
  199:     return aKey.localeCompare(bKey);
  200:   });
  201: }

  201:  * Pega o melhor candidato de close_hour em docs legados.

  203: function getCloseCandidate(d) {
  204:   try {
  205:     const v =
  206:       d?.close_hour ??
  207:       d?.close ??
  208:       d?.close_hour_raw ??
  209:       d?.closeHour ??
  210:       d?.horario ??
  211:       d?.hour ??
  212:       "";
  213:     return String(v ?? "").trim();
  214:   } catch {
  215:     return "";
  216:   }
  217: }
  218: 
  219: /**
  220:  * Normaliza close_hour para SLOT (compatível com import):
  221:  * - PT_RIO: HH:09 => slot HH:00
  222:  * - outros: preserva HH:MM
  223:  */
  224: function normalizeCloseHourForLottery(value, lotteryKey) {
  225:   const lk = upTrim(lotteryKey || "");
  226:   const raw0 = normalizeHHMM(value);
  227:   if (!raw0 || !isHHMM(raw0)) return { raw: "", slot: "" };
  228: 
  229:   if (lk === "PT_RIO") {
  230:     const hh = raw0.slice(0, 2);
  231:     const mm = raw0.slice(3, 5);
  232:     const raw = mm === "09" ? "" : raw0;
  233:     return { raw, slot: `${hh}:00` };
  234:   }
  235: 
  236:   return { raw: raw0, slot: raw0 };
  237: }
  238: 
  239: function hourFromCloseHourSlot(closeHour, lotteryKey) {
  240:   const { slot } = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour, lotteryKey);
  241:   const s0 = String(slot ?? "").trim();
  242:   if (!s0) return null;
  243:   const m = s0.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
  244:   if (!m) return null;
  245:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
  246:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || hh < 0 || hh > 23) return null;
  247:   return pad2(hh);
  248: }

   63:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) ||
   64:       Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))

   66:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || hh < 0 || hh > 23) return null;
   67: 
   68:   return pad2(hh);
   69: }

   48:     typeof input === "object" &&

  808: function drawLooksLikeSlot(draw, slotHH) {
  809:   const hh = hhFromSlotHHMM(slotHH);
  810:   if (!hh) return false;
  811: 
  812:   const v =
  813:     (draw &&
  814:       (draw.close_hour ||
  815:         draw.closeHour ||
  816:         draw.close ||
  817:         draw.hour ||
  818:         draw.slot ||
  819:         draw.time ||
  820:         draw.close_hour_raw)) ??
  821:     "";

  823: 
  824:   if (s.startsWith(hh)) return true;
  825:   if (s.startsWith(`${hh}:`)) return true;
  826:   if (s === hh) return true;
  827:   if (s === `${hh}h`) return true;

  372:  * - Para outros: preserva raw
  373:  *
  374:  * Correção crítica:
  375:  * - A API do PT_RIO costuma devolver HH:09 como "marcação" e NÃO um minuto real.
  376:  * - Portanto, para PT_RIO, se raw terminar em ":09", tratamos como "sem minuto real"
  377:  *   e NÃO persistimos close_hour_raw (vira "").
  378:  *
  379:  * Retorna:
  380:  * - raw: HH:MM (normalizado) OU "" quando for "sintético" (PT_RIO HH:09)
  381:  * - slot: HH:MM (normalizado para o que o sistema deve usar em consultas/ID)
  382:  */

 1010: async function checkSlotCompletion({ date, closeHour, lotteryKey }) {
 1011:   try {
 1012:     const lk = String(lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
 1013:     const targetSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(closeHour || "", lk).slot;
 1014:     if (!targetSlot || !isHHMM(targetSlot)) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
 1015: 
 1016:     // query mínima
 1017:     const snap = await db.collection("draws").where("date", "==", date).get();
 1018:     if (snap.empty) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
 1019: 
 1020:     const refs = [];
 1021:     snap.forEach((doc) => {
 1022:       const d = doc.data() || {};
 1023: 
 1024:       const lkSnake = String(d.lottery_key || "").trim().toUpperCase();
 1025:       const lkCamel = String(d.lotteryKey || "").trim().toUpperCase();
 1026: 
 1027:       if (lkSnake !== lk && lkCamel !== lk) return;
 1028: 
 1029:       // ✅ compara por SLOT normalizado (pega legado "09:09" como "09:00")
 1030:       const storedSlot = normalizeCloseHourForLottery(d.close_hour || "", lk).slot;
 1031:       if (storedSlot !== targetSlot) return;
 1032: 
 1033:       refs.push(doc.ref);
 1034:     });
 1035: 
 1036:     if (!refs.length) return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
 1037: 
 1038:     let complete = 0;
 1039:     for (const ref of refs) {
 1040:       const ok = await checkAlreadyComplete(ref);
 1041:       if (ok) complete += 1;
 1042:     }
 1043: 
 1044:     return { docs: refs.length, complete };
 1045:   } catch {
 1046:     return { docs: 0, complete: 0 };
 1047:   }
 1048: }

--- CONTEXTO: linhas 122 a 147 ---
  122: /**
  123:  * Normaliza "qualquer data" para YYYY-MM-DD (ou null).
  124:  * Aceita:
  125:  * - "YYYY-MM-DD"
  126:  * - "DD/MM/YYYY"
  127:  * - ISO "YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm:ss..."
  128:  * - Timestamp-like { seconds }
  129:  * - Date
  130:  * - Firestore Timestamp (toDate)
  131:  */
  132: function normalizeToYMD(input) {
  133:   if (!input) return null;
  134: 
  135:   // Firestore Timestamp com toDate()
  136:   if (typeof input === "object" && typeof input.toDate === "function") {
  137:     const d = input.toDate();
  138:     if (d instanceof Date && !Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) {
  139:       return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad2(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad2(d.getDate())}`;
  140:     }
  141:   }
  142: 
  143:   // Timestamp-like { seconds } / { _seconds }
  144:   if (
  145:     typeof input === "object" &&
  146:     (Number.isFinite(Number(input.seconds)) || Number.isFinite(Number(input._seconds)))
  147:   ) {

--- CONTEXTO: linhas 223 a 243 ---
  223:     out.push(d);
  224:   }
  225: 
  226:   return out;
  227: }

  236:  *  - hour: "11:00" | "11h" | "11" (opcional)

  260:    - exact  SOMENTE via closeHour
  261:    (elimina ambiguidade e "filtro fantasma")
  262: ========================= */
  263: 
  264: function resolveHourFilter({ closeHour = null, closeHourBucket = null }) {
  265:   const bucket = toHourBucket(closeHourBucket);
  266:   if (bucket) return { kind: "bucket", bucket, hhmm: null };
  267: 
  268:   const hhmm = closeHour ? normalizeHourLike(closeHour) : null;
  269:   if (hhmm) return { kind: "exact", bucket: null, hhmm };
  270: 
  271:   return { kind: null, bucket: null, hhmm: null };
  272: }
  273: 
  274: function drawPassesHourFilter(draw, hourFilter) {
  275:   if (!hourFilter || !hourFilter.kind) return true;
  276: 
  277:   const raw =
  278:     draw?.close_hour ?? draw?.closeHour ?? draw?.hour ?? draw?.hora ?? "";
  279:   const norm = normalizeHourLike(raw);
  280: 
  281:   if (hourFilter.kind === "exact") {
  282:     return norm === normalizeHourLike(hourFilter.hhmm);
  283:   }
  284: 
  285:   if (hourFilter.kind === "bucket") {
  286:     return toHourBucket(norm) === toHourBucket(hourFilter.bucket);
  287:   }
  288: 
  289:   return true;
  290: }
